**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-2-3/pivot-headings/r-sql-2-3.pivot-headings.68d1c68c44.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/pivot-headings-clean/r-fix-expr-sql.pivot-headings-clean.77f1d9ddd8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/pivot-empty-null/r-sql-2-3.pivot-empty-null.382879f2a4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/pivot-cells/r-sql-2-3.pivot-cells.7e6eb2df0a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/pivot-schema-lifetime/r-sql-2-3.pivot-schema-lifetime.27ebaee52e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/pivot-dynaset-requery/r-fix-expr-sql.pivot-dynaset-requery.5b56a896c6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/pivot-limit/r-sql-2-3.pivot-limit.8c88c64eb9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/pivot-clause-forms/r-sql-13b.pivot-clause-forms.2c2071ebfb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/pivot-heading-sort-order/r-sql-13b.pivot-heading-sort-order.77fabece6d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/pivot-in-list-forms/r-sql-13b.pivot-in-list-forms.b2ca805610.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/pivot-parameter-positions/r-sql-13b.pivot-parameter-positions.b3329bc546.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, rendered names).** Numeric headings 1, 1.5, 10, -1 sort numerically and render `-1`, `1`, `1_5`, `10` (rows with 1 and 1 sum to 6); text headings '1', '1.0', '01' render "1", "1_0", "01" and stay distinct (with a fixed IN list in written order, cells 1, 2, 3); 'a' and 'A' merge (11). A leading-space ' 1' and '1' render to one column "1" that holds 4, the ' 1' row's value, not the sum 5: two distinct keys with one rendered name collapse silently to a single column.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/pivot-typed-collisions/r-q-expr-sql.pivot-typed-collisions.68ca3bd422.a1.json`.
**Open.** The mechanism of the ' 1' collision (overwrite or first-name wins); a typed number against a text with the same rendering cannot share one pivot column; locale rendering needs a settings change (T); `PIVOT IN (1.0, 1)` returning zero rows is unexplained.

### R-SQL-3 SQL-META (parameters): declaration, types and value adaptation
**Claim.** Declared types map as Bit 1, Byte 2, Short 3, Long 4 (also Integer, Int), Currency 5, IEEESingle/Single/Real 6, IEEEDouble/Double/Float/Numeric 7, DateTime 8, Binary 9, Text/Char/VarChar/Value 10, LongBinary 11, LongText 12, Guid 15, BigInt 19; `Decimal`, `Memo`, `Counter`, `Boolean`, `Variant`, `TinyInt` are refused (3139). A trailing `;` is required after PARAMETERS; two PARAMETERS clauses fail 3129; duplicate names are accepted at CreateQueryDef and fail at `Parameters.Count` (3060); 64- and 65-character names open. A parameter in an ON clause is refused (3296), every other position opens. Assigned values adapt: Long and Short round half to even (2.5 gives 2, 2.6 gives 3, 3.5 gives 4, -2.5 gives -2), Byte rejects -2.5, 255.5, 300 and text (3421), Bit becomes -1 for any non-Null number, DateTime rejects "abc" and "5" (3421) but takes I4 70000, Text keeps text longer than its declared length (10 and 26 characters read in full), Currency/Single/Double/DateTime keep the value; a rejected assignment leaves the old value; an unassigned parameter fails 3061 at OpenRecordset; Null makes `x = p` Null and `p Is Null` -1. `QueryDef.Fields` of a saved parameter query is readable before assignment.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-declaration-syntax/r-sql-13b.params-declaration-syntax.d11177e387.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-declared-type-names/r-sql-13b.params-declared-type-names.def4e28133.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-positions/r-sql-13b.params-positions.5c6047d0f4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-count-limit/r-sql-13b.params-count-limit.dd1f5a3b5e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/params-values-rounding-and-length/r-fix-expr-sql.params-values-rounding-and-length.99dc9925f0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/parameters-values-set/r-fix-expr-sql.parameters-values-set.55c1458fde.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/nz-free-forms/r-fix-expr-sql.nz-free-forms.4105e37dd6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/params-saved/r-sql-2-3.params-saved.a78be0cf53.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/params-implicit-temp/r-sql-2-3.params-implicit-temp.38ef68ab0e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-value-readback/r-sql-13b.params-value-readback.1f3eed4e11.a1.json`.
**Open.** `Parameters.Count` fails when 255 or more parameters are all used (unexplained). Parameter lifecycle on a held recordset is in the EXPR-BIND note.

### R-SQL-2 ACT-UPD-01, ACT-UPD-03, ACT-UPD-04, ACT-INS-02, ACT-INS-03: assignment views, joined targets, counts, INSERT SELECT
**Claim.** UPDATE assignments read the original row: `SET V=W, W=V` swaps in either textual order and `V=W+1, W=V+1, S=V&'-'&W` uses the old values; two assignments to one field fail 3063 with nothing changed; `SET N=(SELECT...)` and `SET ID=ID+1` (collision) are refused. A join UPDATE counts joined occurrences (P1 matched by two children counts 3), `SET P.Name=C.Tag` leaves the last child's value, DISTINCTROW counts 2, a DISTINCT saved query as target fails 3073, LEFT JOIN with the outer side as target fails 3058, a two-target SET changes both tables, dbConsistent/dbInconsistent both succeed. RecordsAffected counts no-op assignments (`SET V=V` gives 4), is not counted for cascades, is set on the QueryDef that executed and `Database.RecordsAffected` stays 0 until `Database.Execute` runs. INSERT SELECT from the target table is finite (4 rows gives 8 rows; appended rows do not re-enter), a SELECT without a column list maps by name, a short select fails 3314, one extra value 3346; omitted column gets its default (5) and explicit Null stays Null; insertion through an updatable saved query or a join succeeds.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-upd-assign/r-sql-2-3.act-upd-assign.ac3d38f712.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/update-assignment-reseeded/r-fix-expr-sql.update-assignment-reseeded.92fbe9f025.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-upd-join/r-sql-2-3.act-upd-join.9e423c7a63.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-records-affected/r-sql-2-3.act-records-affected.53ac1b81d4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-cascade/r-sql-2-3.act-cascade.9c205eff72.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-qdef-routes/r-sql-2-3.act-qdef-routes.297136c51e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-ins-self/r-sql-2-3.act-ins-self.29656dc32f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-ins-mapping/r-sql-2-3.act-ins-mapping.9e73b54376.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-ins-defaults/r-sql-2-3.act-ins-defaults.f84f17d4f5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/sql-insert-values-forms/r-def-expr-sql.sql-insert-values-forms.7ffaec6fa3.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, joined target layout).** A joined `UPDATE PJ INNER JOIN Cx ON PJ.ID = Cx.PID SET PJ.Name = Cx.Tag` with two children leaves the value of the last child in scan (insertion) order: children inserted 10 then 11 leave "c11", inserted 11 then 10 leave "c10", with and without an index on the foreign key; RecordsAffected is 2 each time.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/act-upd-join-layout/r-q-expr-sql.act-upd-join-layout.105930ad58.a1.json`.
**Open.** Joined target with an effectful expression needs a host route; stale `RecordsAffected` after DDL; reentry needs a host route.

### R-SQL-2 SQL-CONTINUE: row failure without dbFailOnError
**Claim.** Without dbFailOnError a failing candidate is skipped without an error and later candidates continue, for unique (3022), validation (3316), relationship (3200/3201), required (3314) and conversion (3464) failures; the rows that changed are counted in RecordsAffected and Errors stays unchanged (a single-row failing INSERT gives RecordsAffected 0 and no error). A locked row (second Workspace pessimistic Edit) gives success with RecordsAffected 0; with dbFailOnError 3188. A conversion failure stores Null for that row and counts it not. These hold on MDB for the unique and lock cases.
**Conditions.** DAO Database.Execute and QueryDef.Execute, ACCDB and MDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-fail-unique/r-sql-2-3.act-fail-unique.8d251e639e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/mdb-fail-unique/r-sql-2-3.mdb-fail-unique.29f2e5ac58.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-fail-validation/r-sql-2-3.act-fail-validation.6ad741c4ef.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fail-relationship-update-isolated/r-fix-expr-sql.fail-relationship-update-isolated.1f9c1bdf98.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fail-insert-required-cross-table/r-fix-expr-sql.fail-insert-required-cross-table.010320f5e9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-fail-conversion/r-sql-2-3.act-fail-conversion.0dcbbf3a73.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-lock-blocked/r-sql-2-3.act-lock-blocked.c1f4d15854.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/mdb-lock-blocked/r-sql-2-3.mdb-lock-blocked.833f67bbeb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/route-action-query-expressions/r-expr-1b.route-action-query-expressions.92a71c40cb.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, locks).** A pessimistic Edit by a second Workspace on any one row of a 3-row table (ID 2, ID 1 or ID 3), or on all three, blocks the whole UPDATE: without dbFailOnError the call succeeds with RecordsAffected 0 and no row changes, including the unblocked rows; with dbFailOnError it fails 3188 with no row changed. A successful no-flag Execute neither clears nor adds to Errors (the marker's 3376 entry is still the only one). The lock therefore covers a page (all rows here share one), not a row.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/cont-blocked-positions/r-q-expr-sql.cont-blocked-positions.ba95f08bc3.a1.json`.
**Open.** Skip-and-continue for a locked candidate needs candidate rows on different pages (a multi-page table with the blocked row apart from the others): not asked, status stays F for that part.

### R-PAR-1 PAR-FOLD: Currency, Decimal and Single accumulation
**Claim.** Sum and Avg over Currency overflow at OpenRecordset (error 6) whenever the exact running total leaves the Currency range, even if the final sum would be in range (g1, g2, g4..g6, g11, g12), and succeed at the boundary values (g3 922337203685477.5807, g8 0.9999); Var/StDev over Currency overflow in 10 of 12 groups. `Sum(CDbl(c))` of the same group is Double 922337203685477.6. A Decimal column sums as Decimal at full precision: 28 nines + 1 - 28 nines is 1; 28 nines + 1 and a group of 28 nines overflows with 3761; Sum(x*1) stays Decimal; Avg is Decimal with the scale needed (0.3333333333333333333333333333); Var/StDev are Double or overflow (6). Single sums are Double (g3 6.805646932770577E+38, no overflow).
**Records.** `evidence/r-par-1/fold-currency-sum-groups/r-par-1.fold-currency-sum-groups.ac7abebfe6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-currency-avg-minmax/r-par-1.fold-currency-avg-minmax.4b4330c5d6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-currency-var-stdev/r-par-1.fold-currency-var-stdev.decaedc844.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-currency-argument-forms/r-par-1.fold-currency-argument-forms.efd4183936.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-decimal-sum-groups/r-par-1.fold-decimal-sum-groups.5dc68e238f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fold-decimal-28-digits/r-fix-expr-sql.fold-decimal-28-digits.430d58abfd.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fold-decimal-avg-var-forms/r-fix-expr-sql.fold-decimal-avg-var-forms.04deca2ebd.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-single-groups/r-par-1.fold-single-groups.12570a2f84.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/agg-sum-boundary/r-sql-13b.agg-sum-boundary.d88bac0645.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/agg-sum-boundary-per-group/r-fix-expr-sql.agg-sum-boundary-per-group.208b46ede7.a1.json`.

**Open.** Whether Currency accumulates as exact 64-bit integers or in a wider intermediate is not distinguished by these records. The Double order is in the Double fold order note.

### R-PAR-1 PAR-ORDER: scan order, grouping order and ties
**Claim.** An unordered table scan returns insertion order, not primary-key order (OT5/OT2/OT3/OT4 identical, filtered scans identical); a dynaset of the table returns primary-key order while snapshot and forward-only return insertion order; GROUP BY and DISTINCT output is ascending by key; UNION returns ascending distinct values and UNION ALL keeps arm order; ORDER BY ties come out in a deterministic order that is not insertion order, identical across tables with different indexes, and it changes after DELETE/INSERT/UPDATE; TOP n and TOP n PERCENT keep the whole tie group (TOP 5 over a join returned 24 rows). The order of join duplicate matches depends on the table pairing and indexes.
**Records.** `evidence/r-par-1/order-scan-storage-order/r-par-1.order-scan-storage-order.7c47b25e2c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-scan-filter-routes/r-par-1.order-scan-filter-routes.83879b1a70.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-recordset-types/r-par-1.order-recordset-types.e232632274.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-groupby-output-order/r-par-1.order-groupby-output-order.b48c25d6be.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-groupby-output-order-mdb/r-par-1.order-groupby-output-order-mdb.ef422953ef.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/order-distinct-full-read/r-fix-expr-sql.order-distinct-full-read.0d563de4a9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-union-without-order-by/r-par-1.order-union-without-order-by.8c1f7f4de4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-ties-order-by-k/r-par-1.order-ties-order-by-k.29d06704d8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-after-delete-insert-update/r-par-1.order-after-delete-insert-update.6d90c07ccb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-join-duplicate-matches/r-par-1.order-join-duplicate-matches.eb3683de21.a1.json`.
**Open.** The tie order itself has no explanation (a sort algorithm artefact); no claim about it beyond "deterministic, not insertion order".

### R-PAR-1 PAR-FOLD, R-SQL-1 SQL-AGG-ORDER, SQL-AGG-FIRST: Double Sum follows the feed order, which an index maintained during insert scrambles
**Claim.** Sum and Avg of Double are a strictly sequential left-to-right accumulation in the order the rows reach the aggregate. In all eleven tables the Sum for group 3 (2^53, 4096 ones, -2^53 by id 8195, 8196..12291, 12292) equals the sum recomputed offline in IEEE doubles from the retained scan order: FL, FLD (index on d only), FLN (primary key only), FLX (no key), FLC (make-table copy) and the three post-load tables (index on g, on d, on both created after the rows exist) read group 3 in id order (8195..12292) and sum 0; FLR (copy inserted ORDER BY id DESC) reads 12292 down to 8195 and sums 4096; FLI (indexes on g and d built before INSERT SELECT) reads group 3 as 9701..10282, 8195..9700, 10283..12292 and sums 584; FLG (index on g only, built before the insert) reads 8343..9506, 8195..8342, 9507..12292 and sums 1164. WHERE g = 3 and GROUP BY g give the same Sum; First and Last of id equal the first and last ids of the retained order (FLI 9701 and 12292); the whole-table scan starts 1..40 in every table except FLR (12292 downward). So an index on g that was maintained during the insert delivers an equal-key run in a rotated order of id runs, an index on g built after the load, an index on d only, no index, a make-table copy and a reversed insert give plain scan order, and the Sum is whatever the sequential sum of that order is. The earlier 1360 for FLI did not repeat: the rebuilt FLI (same recipe) gives 584 with a different rotation, so the rotation point depends on the build, not the recipe.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, ACCDB, one process; offline sums recomputed in awk (IEEE doubles) from the arrays retained by GetRows.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FL/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FL.afd432826c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLI/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLI.b10577fe82.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLG/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLG.6d952e77cf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLD/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLD.6838b79f00.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLN/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLN.f8d7f77466.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLX/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLX.5a76eac0b6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLR/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLR.be5581ea58.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLC/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLC.6afd4dd7a1.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-post-g/r-q-expr-sql.fold-post-g.3a471b968a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-post-d/r-q-expr-sql.fold-post-d.ea93514e2a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-post-both/r-q-expr-sql.fold-post-both.79ca5ef774.a1.json`; earlier: `evidence/r-par-1/fold-double-large-groups/r-par-1.fold-double-large-groups.b518c9662a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fold-double-routes-materialized/r-fix-expr-sql.fold-double-routes-materialized.765f09d5d6.a1.json`.
**Open.** The FG case (4096 groups, no index on g: 1164 groups summed 1 and 2932 summed 0 in GROUP BY output, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-double-many-groups/r-par-1.fold-double-many-groups.47b07be2be.a1.json`) was not re-asked with a retained feed order, so the many-group GROUP BY feed order is unexplained; why an index maintained during insert rotates the equal-key run; reproducibility of the rotation across rebuilds.

### R-SQL-1 SQL-AGG-NUMERIC: Var, VarP, StDev and StDevP come from single-pass Double sums
**Claim.** ACE computes the variance family from Σx and Σx² accumulated in Double, not from deviations. For 1e9 + (4, 7, 13, 16) (exact sample variance 30) ACE returns Var 170.66666666666666 and VarP 128; for 1e15 + (1, 2, 3) (exact 1) 187649984473770.66 and 125099989649180.44; for 1e8 + (0.1, 0.2, 0.3) (exact 0.01) 2.6666666666666665 and 1.7777777777777777; for 1e16 + (0, 2, 4) (exact 4) 24019198012642644 and 16012798675095096. These magnitudes are reproduced exactly offline in IEEE doubles by (n·Σx² − (Σx)²) / (n·(n−1)) for Var and / n² for VarP; ACE returns them positive although that expression is negative for these inputs (the sign handling is not determined). StDev and StDevP are their square roots (13.063945294843617 and 11.313708498984761 for the first group). 1e150 · (1, 2, 3) gives Var 1.0000000000000006E+300 (exact 1e300) and 1e154 · (1, 2, 3) fails with Overflow (6) at OpenRecordset because the squares leave the Double range although the variance is representable.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, ACCDB, Double column, GROUP BY and WHERE g = k.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/agg-var-recurrence/r-q-expr-sql.agg-var-recurrence.d29f892304.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/agg-numeric-per-group/r-fix-expr-sql.agg-numeric-per-group.4cfc915b42.a1.json`.
**Open.** Sign handling; accumulation order for sums that cancel (the order dependence is in the Double fold order note); Currency, Decimal and Date inputs.

### R-SQL-1 SQL-SUB-03, SQL-SUB-04: correlation names and EXISTS shapes
**Claim.** A correlated count through an alias (`e2.Id <= Ev.Id`) gives 1, 2, 3; when the inner table has the outer table's name (`FROM Ev WHERE Id <= Ev.Id`) the inner name shadows the outer and every row gives 3; three nested correlated EXISTS levels return all rows; EXISTS over `SELECT DISTINCT` and over a GROUP BY subquery returns all rows; EXISTS ignores a failing projection (`1/x.Denominator` with Denominator 0 at Id 2 returns Ids 1, 2, 3); `EXISTS (SELECT TOP 1 x.Id FROM Ev AS x WHERE x.Id = Ev.Id)` returned only Id 1, so TOP in a correlated subquery does not behave per outer row. Earlier: an alias `L2` with `L.id` inside failed 3061, and a declared parameter `lid` won over column `R.lid`.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, ACCDB, ANSI-89 syntax.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/sub-corr-shapes/r-q-expr-sql.sub-corr-shapes.e9e8b6b82d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/sub-correlated/r-sql-1.sub-correlated.df79dc512e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/sub-exists-demand/r-sql-1.sub-exists-demand.2e3c35d3b4.a1.json`.
**Open.** Why TOP 1 returns once (a TOP applied before the correlation?); nested parameter-name reuse; callbacks in the child need a host route (SQL-SUB-05, T).

### R-SQL-2 SQL-ACT-FAIL: failure disposition with dbFailOnError by statement kind
**Claim.** With dbFailOnError a failure in a later candidate leaves a different state by statement kind. UPDATE stops at the failing candidate and keeps the earlier changes: unique (3022) changed IDs 1 and 2 (1 to 11, 2 to 12) and left ID 3 and 4; conversion (3464, `CLng(S)` failing at ID 3) set V for IDs 1 and 2 and left 3 and 4 Null; validation (3316) and relationship (3201) behave the same in the earlier records. INSERT ... SELECT with a Null for a required field at the third source row (3314) appends no row; DELETE whose third candidate has a child (3200) deletes none (IDs 1 and 2, which had no child, remain). RecordsAffected reads 0 after each failure although UPDATE rows changed. Without the flag each skips the failing candidate and continues (SQL-CONTINUE note).
**Conditions.** DAO Database.Execute outside an explicit transaction, ACCDB, four candidates, the failing one third.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fail-late-candidate/r-q-expr-sql.fail-late-candidate.7b269cfdc0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-fail-unique/r-sql-2-3.act-fail-unique.8d251e639e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-fail-validation/r-sql-2-3.act-fail-validation.6ad741c4ef.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-fail-conversion/r-sql-2-3.act-fail-conversion.0dcbbf3a73.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fail-relationship-update-isolated/r-fix-expr-sql.fail-relationship-update-isolated.1f9c1bdf98.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fail-insert-required-cross-table/r-fix-expr-sql.fail-insert-required-cross-table.010320f5e9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-trans-frames/r-sql-2-3.act-trans-frames.636a969421.a1.json`.
**Open.** Whether the INSERT and DELETE reversal is an implicit transaction or a pre-check is not determined; prepared-definition changes and injected I/O, TEMP and allocation failures need other tooling (T).

### R-SQL-2 ACT-SEL-01, ACT-SEL-02: candidate stability for key-moving updates
**Claim.** An UPDATE that moves the key it selects on changes each qualifying row once, with or without an index. Rows V = 10, 20, 20, 20, 30, 30, 5: `V = V + 100 WHERE V BETWEEN 10 AND 30` affects 6 rows (110, 120 x3, 130 x2; the 5 stays), `V = V - 100 WHERE V >= 100` 6 rows back, `V = V + 5 WHERE V >= 10` 6 rows; the indexed and the unindexed table agree. On 4096 rows (k = n) `k = k + 100000 WHERE k >= 0` affects 4096 rows (min 100001, max 104096) and `k = k - 1 WHERE k > 100` affects 4096 (min 100000, max 104095), with and without an index on k: no row is revisited or skipped.
**Conditions.** DAO Database.Execute with dbFailOnError, ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/act-sel-repeated-keys/r-q-expr-sql.act-sel-repeated-keys.9ccfed59e0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/act-sel-leaf-split/r-q-expr-sql.act-sel-leaf-split.148d46c5a0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-sel-range/r-sql-2-3.act-sel-range.dcd16645d1.a1.json`.
**Open.** That the 4096-entry index split its leaves was not verified (file bytes, W-04); variable rows growing into overflow bodies and relocated roots; callbacks that change unseen rows (T).

### R-SQL-2 ACT-INS-01: AutoNumber consumption by failed inserts
**Claim.** A failed insert consumes an AutoNumber: with An(ID Counter, Req NOT NULL, U unique) and dbFailOnError, ('a',1) gets ID 1, ('b',1) (3022) consumes 2, ('c',2) gets 3, (Null,3) (3314) consumes 4, ('d',3) gets 5. An explicit ID that collides sets the seed to that ID + 1 even though the insert fails: INSERT with ID 1 (3022) is followed by a plain ('f',10) that gets ID 2 (a gap is reused) and a plain ('g',11) that fails 3022 because the seed (3) is taken.
**Conditions.** DAO Database.Execute with dbFailOnError, ACCDB, one sequence.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/act-ins-autonumber/r-q-expr-sql.act-ins-autonumber.0ac261a7d4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-ins-defaults/r-sql-2-3.act-ins-defaults.f84f17d4f5.a1.json`.
**Open.** The explicit-ID variant was asked once, in sequence; physical re-preparation must not consume a second value (T).

### R-SQL-2 SQL-OWNERACCESS: WITH OWNERACCESS OPTION under default Admin
**Claim.** The clause is accepted and changes nothing observable under the default Admin account with a blank password and no workgroup file, on ACCDB and on a Jet 4 MDB: a direct OpenRecordset, a temporary QueryDef, a saved QueryDef and an INSERT ... SELECT ... WITH OWNERACCESS OPTION run as without it (rows 1,10 and 2,20; RecordsAffected 2). The QueryDef SQL keeps the clause (re-rendered with line breaks and a trailing `;`), Type is 0 and Updatable is -1 with and without it.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/act-owneraccess-routes/r-q-expr-sql.act-owneraccess-routes.1e75ddae31.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-owneraccess/r-sql-2-3.act-owneraccess.8885d6a8da.a1.json`.
**Open.** Independent reopen and native bytes (R-CAT-1); named logon, workgroup file and permission checks stay T (out of scope).

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-11, PAT-16: counted U+0000 and pattern reuse
**Claim.** Counted U+0000 is preserved in stored subjects and in parameter values: Len of S = 'A', 'A'+NUL, 'A'+NUL+'B', 'A'+NUL+'C' is 1, 2, 3, 3. For pattern 'A', `S = pp` is true for 'A' and 'A'+NUL (a trailing NUL is ignored by `=`) and false for the longer values, `S LIKE pp` only for 'A'. For 'A'+NUL, `=` is true for 'A' and 'A'+NUL and LIKE only for 'A'+NUL. 'A'+NUL+'B' matches only row 3 and 'A'+NUL+'C' only row 4 under both. 'A'+NUL+'*' matches rows 2, 3 and 4 under LIKE and none under `=`. One QueryDef whose pp is assigned 'A'+NUL+'B', then 'A'+NUL+'C', then 'A', with a newly opened recordset after each assignment, returns each value's own result: no stale reuse of an earlier pattern.
**Conditions.** DAO QueryDef with `PARAMETERS pp Text(255)`, snapshot, ACCDB; a NUL inside the statement text truncates it (3075, `r-fix-expr-sql/nul-pattern-requery-no-argument`).
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/pat-11-16-counted-nul/r-q-expr-sql.pat-11-16-counted-nul.027f707001.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/nul-pattern-requery-no-argument/r-fix-expr-sql.nul-pattern-requery-no-argument.e8629e506b.a1.json`.
**Open.** Reuse through Requery cannot be asked (crashes ACE); the cache identity of a pattern is not observable through DAO.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-BIND: parameter and schema changes seen by a fresh open
**Claim.** A fresh OpenRecordset of a saved QueryDef reads the current parameter value and the current SQL, and a recordset opened earlier keeps its own values. QBp (`PARAMETERS pl Long; SELECT pl + 1`): pl = 1 gives 2, pl = 2 and a new open gives 3, the first recordset re-read after MoveFirst still gives 2; after QBp.SQL is replaced by `pl + 10` a new open gives 12 and both held recordsets still read 2 and 3. Saved queries re-resolve at every open: ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN fails 3211 (cannot lock) while a recordset of the table is open and succeeds after it is closed; then `SELECT *` opens with 3 fields (the new column Null) while `SELECT ID, A` keeps 2; after DROP COLUMN A `SELECT *` opens with 2 fields and `SELECT ID, A` fails 3061 (A becomes an unresolved parameter); after DROP TABLE and a recreate with A as Text both open again.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB, snapshots; no Requery.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/bind-param-fresh-open/r-q-expr-sql.bind-param-fresh-open.e496cff6f2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/bind-schema-change/r-q-expr-sql.bind-schema-change.50a1c085f0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/parameter-lifecycle-no-requery-argument/r-fix-expr-sql.parameter-lifecycle-no-requery-argument.b096e7d778.a1.json`.
**Open.** Cache hit and miss are not observable; Requery (crash); the declared parameter's identity after SQL replacement; the II-04 Row A decoder differential is CalcDb's work.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-DOMAIN: lookup order, Null handling and visibility inside one Workspace
**Claim.** DLookup and DFirst return the first matching row in scan (insertion) order and DLast the last, not the key order: rows (id, g, v) inserted (3,1,30), (1,1,10), (2,1,20), (4,2,40), (5,1,50), (6,2,60) give for g = 1 DLookup "30", DFirst "30", DLast "50", DMin "10", DMax "50" and DLookup without criteria "30", identically for Dd (primary key on id), DdI (also an index on g) and DdN (no key): the results are Text. DSum ignores Null: an all-Null group and a group with no rows give Null, (5, Null) gives 5, the whole table 9, `DSum('v * 2', ...)` 8; DCount('v') counts non-Null values (0 for the all-Null group and for no rows); DAvg of an all-Null group is Null; `DSum('1 / 0', ...)` fails 11; an absent criteria field fails 3061 "Too few parameters". Inside one Workspace a domain function sees an uncommitted INSERT (DCount 1), the row disappears after Rollback (0) and stays after CommitTrans; an AddNew edit buffer is invisible until Update (1, then 2).
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB, one process; domain calls inside a snapshot SELECT.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/domain-lookup-order/r-q-expr-sql.domain-lookup-order.45d0c945db.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/domain-dsum-null-empty/r-q-expr-sql.domain-dsum-null-empty.2579b92f64.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/domain-txn-visibility/r-q-expr-sql.domain-txn-visibility.90af78bc65.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-domain-aggregate-values/r-def-expr-sql.expr-domain-aggregate-values.1f10ca6730.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-domain-criteria-and-failures/r-def-expr-sql.expr-domain-criteria-and-failures.6cc34da748.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/domain-per-row-no-nz/r-fix-expr-sql.domain-per-row-no-nz.993dcfba68.a1.json`.
**Open.** Another Database object, a separate Workspace and a competing process, and the Access form namespace (T); larger tables and key order; the DSum result type; `DLookup(..., '')` returned Null only because the first row's v is Null.

## R-ADO-1, R-ENC-1, R-LINK-1, R-COMPLEX-1, R-REBUILD-1, R-OLEDB-1 and R-ODBC-1

### R-ADO-1 ADO-CONN: provider resolution, construction orders, open and post-open rules
- **Claim**: A new ADO Connection reports Provider "MSDASQL"; ACE is used only if `Provider` (property or connection-string key) names `Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0` before Open, and a Data Source is required (none gives 0x80040E4D "Authentication failed"). A bare path without Provider is read as an ODBC DSN (MSDASQL, "Data source name too long"). Reading `Properties` on a fresh Connection pins MSDASQL (a later Provider assignment gives 0x800A0C94); assigning Provider after nothing, State, ConnectionString or Mode does not. Accepted orders: Provider then Open(path), ConnectionString then Open, Data Source via `Properties("Data Source")`, a duplicated Provider key, whitespace around `Mode = Read`, quoted values (the Provider getter keeps the quotes until Open). An unknown key (`CalcDbUnknownOption`) gives 0x80004005 "Could not find installable ISAM". After Open, ConnectionString is expanded (`User ID=Admin;Data Source=...;Jet OLEDB:Engine Type=6;...`); while open, ConnectionString, Provider and Mode assignments give 0x800A0E79, CursorLocation/CommandTimeout/IsolationLevel/Attributes are accepted, and Data Source or Mode as Properties give 0x80040E21. After Close ConnectionString reads "" and assigned values persist, and Open with no new settings gives 0x80040E4D. UID "Admin"/"" opens; another user or any password gives 0x80040E4D (SQLState 3028).
- **Conditions**: ACCDB and MDB fixtures, read and read/write modes, relative, upper-case, forward-slash and `.\` Data Source spellings open; `\\?\inputs\...` (relative behind the prefix) gives 3043.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-open-alt-orders/r-str-ado-act.ado-open-alt-orders.0648fd8b02.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-connstring-keys/r-str-ado-act.ado-connstring-keys.b6c2e4bd38.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-cs-bare-path/r-ado-1b.conn-cs-bare-path.683780018e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-datasource-spellings/r-ado-1b.conn-datasource-spellings.5a2c81638f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-open-args/r-ado-1b.conn-open-args.f9c16cb1f2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-set-while-open/r-ado-1b.conn-set-while-open.5efbd0c7f5.a1.json`. Discriminators: `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-provider-pin-isolated/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-provider-pin-isolated.aa5101744f.a1.json` (the Properties read, not Provider/State/ConnectionString/Mode, pins MSDASQL; this removes the order confound of `ado-open-provider-property`), `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-open-provider-property-ace-first/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-open-provider-property-ace-first.220390f412.a1.json` (ACE inventoried: 31 properties closed, 101 open, 31 after Close).
- **Open**: `File Name=` (UDL) and an absolute `\\?\C:\...` spelling (the builder cannot concatenate or absolutize a path); empty-valued and aliased keys; ADO-CONN on a `.NET Framework 4.8` caller.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-PROVIDER: effective provider routes (ACE, default, MSPersist)
- **Claim**: Three routes were asked and each is stable: ACE (explicit, or selected through the Provider property) opens the file; an omitted or default provider is MSDASQL and reaches the ODBC Driver Manager, which fails (IM002 "Data source name not found", or IM010 for a bare path); `Provider="MSPersist"` reads back "MSPersist.1" and serves persisted files (`Recordset.Open(file, conn)` and `Connection.Execute(file)` return the 4 saved rows). Each failed non-ACE open leaves State 0 and one Errors entry (Number -2147467259, SQLState IM002).
- **Conditions**: ADO only; no DSN or driver exists on the machine, so every MSDASQL attempt fails at the Driver Manager.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-provider-non-ace/r-str-ado-act.ado-provider-non-ace.9a0ec48d46.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-persist/r-str-ado-act.ado-persist.4ad2e7d2b5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-cs-bare-path/r-ado-1b.conn-cs-bare-path.683780018e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-provider-pin-isolated/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-provider-pin-isolated.aa5101744f.a1.json`.
- **Open**: named non-ACE providers (Jet 4, unknown, empty names) and conflicting duplicate Provider keys are not asked; new probes may only open ACE connections (prompt rule), and `ado-provider-missing-names` stays off pending the owner (ACT-MISSING). One earlier ODBC attempt without a Dbq returned "Operation was canceled" and the record does not say whether a prompt appeared (`evidence/r-oledb-odbc/odbc-path-free-connections/r-oledb-odbc.odbc-path-free-connections.5e580a45cb.a1.json`).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-SESSION: sessions follow the Connection object, not the string
- **Claim**: Recordsets and Commands opened on one Connection return that same Connection object from `ActiveConnection`; `Connection.Close` leaves them at State 0 and `Command.ActiveConnection` returns a null interface pointer; a second Connection to the same file stays open and records its own errors (SQLState 3000 from its own bad statement). Rollback scope is the Connection: ADO changes made inside a transaction revert for Connection.Execute, Command.Execute, keyset Update and client UpdateBatch, and a released (not closed) transaction connection leaves a second ADO connection and DAO reading the old values.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB, writable ACE connection; same-process connections.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-session-writable/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-session-writable.99362e12b0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-session/r-str-ado-act.ado-session.f1e2e94cae.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/disconnect-close-connection/r-ado-1b.disconnect-close-connection.ea2b9594c3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/txn-rollback-scope-objects/r-ado-1b.txn-rollback-scope-objects.64de5c2b49.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/txn-release-without-close/r-ado-1b.txn-release-without-close.efe977c816.a1.json`.
- **Open**: an implicit Connection created from a connection string (needs a concatenated string, T); equal strings on two Connection objects against each other's errors and transactions.


### R-ADO-1 ADO-ASYNC: ADO async flags return early on this provider (events not asked)
- **Claim**: With the ACE provider ADO returns before completion for `adAsyncExecute` and `adAsyncConnect`. `Connection.Execute` of a seven-way cross join (10^7 rows) with `adAsyncExecute` returns a Recordset at once with Connection State 5 and Recordset State 4; both stay pending at 100 ms in two successive runs, are still pending at 1000 ms in the second run, and are open (State 1, value 10000000) by 3000 ms in both. An UPDATE with `adAsyncExecute|adExecuteNoRecords` returns a null recordset with Connection State 5 and State 1 at 200 ms; `Recordset.Open("K", adCmdTable|adAsyncExecute)` returns State 4 and State 1 at 200 ms; `adCmdTableDirect|adAsyncExecute` is refused 0x800A0BB9. `Connection.Open(..., adAsyncConnect)` returns State 2 and reads State 1 at 50 ms, repeated on two connections; for an absent file State 2 is followed by 0x80004005 (3024) on the State read at 50 ms, State 0 with one Errors entry (3024) at 500 and 2000 ms, and Close then fails 0x800A0E78. Cancel (earlier record) returned 0 and did not abort the query.
- **Conditions**: ADO over ACE only, ACCDB, no event sink. The early State is what ADO reports; the records cannot separate an ACE async path from an ADO worker thread.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/async-execute-polled/r-q-ado-misc.async-execute-polled.98bd8802aa.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/async-open-connect-polled/r-q-ado-misc.async-open-connect-polled.a2ea66ba74.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/async-flag-matrix/r-q-ado-misc.async-flag-matrix.6df9172641.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-async/r-str-ado-act.ado-async.2bd4dbabf6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-open-async-connect/r-ado-1b.conn-open-async-connect.dfc04d7265.a1.json`. Repeat: both timing results above were observed twice (execute, connect).
- **Decision flag**: by the card text a proven early return is an initial-scope/architecture conflict with D-CALL-1, for the owner; this note records the observation only, not a classification.
- **Open**: events and callback thread (ADO-EVENT, T); `adAsyncFetch` and `adAsyncFetchNonBlocking` on a source slow enough to show a pending state (the 10^6 join was not).


### R-ADO-1 ADO-PROPS: dynamic Property inventory by connection phase
- **Claim**: With Provider set to ACE the Connection's Properties collection holds 31 entries before Open (data-source initialisation group: Cache Authentication, Password, User ID, Data Source, Mode, Locale Identifier, OLE DB Services, the `Jet OLEDB:` options, ...), 101 after Open (the 70 provider-information entries such as Active Sessions, DBMS Name, Provider Name, Isolation Levels, Transaction DDL, `Autocommit Isolation Levels` and the runtime `Jet OLEDB:` timing options are added), and 31 again after Close, in the same order. Between closed and open only `Jet OLEDB:Engine Type` changes value (0 to 6 for ACCDB); after Close the Data Source entry reads "" (and the other 30 are unchanged). After a failed Open (absent file) the collection has 31 entries, State 0, ConnectionString "", Data Source "", Engine Type 0, Mode 16, one Errors entry (3024). Lookup by name (`Item("Jet OLEDB:Engine Type")`: Value 6, Type 3, Attributes 1537) and by ordinal (`Item(5)`: "Column Definition", 1) works on an open connection; `Properties.Refresh` leaves retained wrappers and their Values readable. A Recordset and a Command each expose 88 properties (Command: before any Execute). A wrapper retained across Open or Close fails 0x800A0D5C (draft record `ado-open-provider-property-ace-first`): wrappers are not reattached by name.
- **Conditions**: ADO Connection over ACE, ACCDB, Provider assigned before any Properties read (a Properties read on a fresh Connection pins MSDASQL, see ADO-CONN).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/props-conn-inventory/r-q-ado-misc.props-conn-inventory.ecdc06bcba.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/props-failed-open/r-q-ado-misc.props-failed-open.df6a44df10.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/props-lookup-refresh/r-q-ado-misc.props-lookup-refresh.0b8d3b783c.a1.json`; discriminator for the retained-wrapper rule: `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-open-provider-property-ace-first/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-open-provider-property-ace-first.220390f412.a1.json`; context `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/prop-connection-inventory/r-oledb-odbc.prop-connection-inventory.56c9930ca5.a1.json`.
- **Open**: Type/Attributes of every entry was captured in the record but not tabulated here; property inventories of Recordset/Command after Execute.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-PARAM: CreateParameter, derived metadata, value coercion and positional binding
- **Claim**: Binding is by position in every form asked. A saved query `QPair(pA, pB)` as adCmdStoredProc exposes no derived parameters (Count 0, `Item(0)` 0x800A0CC1); appending `pA`=2 then `pB`=4 returns rows 2..4; appending `pB`=4 first and `pA`=2 second returns no rows (names do not matter, position does); parameters named `x` and `y` in declared order return rows 2..4; one parameter only fails 0x80040E10 "Too few parameters. Expected 2." (SQLState 3061); a third surplus parameter is accepted and ignored. A text command with two `?` markers bound `hi`=4 then `lo`=2 returns no rows with NamedParameters False and with True (names cannot select). Parameter creation: `Append` of an untyped parameter, or of Type 202 with Size 0 or omitted, fails 0x800A0E7C; Size -1 and Size 100 (Type 203) append; Type 9999 and Direction 99 fail 0x800A0BB9. A `?` text derives Name "?", Type 202, Size 510, Precision 255 (Count 2 on first access, 0 after Refresh in one record); changing CommandText re-derives. For a Long parameter in `L = ?` the tags I2, UI1, R8, R4, CY, DATE, "20", "2e1" and " 20 " select ids 2 and 4; R8 20.5 reads as 20; BOOL -1 and Null select nothing; "0x14", "" and "abc" and a byte array fail 0x800A0D5D and keep the old value; out-of-range numerics fail 0x8002000A at the put; Name changes after Append fail 0x800A0C93; Output/InputOutput/ReturnValue on a `?` command fail 0x80040E21; a Command with no connection fails 0x800A0E7D. Decimal parameters: String and Double 3.125 read back DECIMAL scale 3, Currency scale 4, Long scale 0.
- **Conditions**: ADO Command, ACCDB, ACE provider; values bound at Append time.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/param-saved-declared-order/r-q-ado-misc.param-saved-declared-order.23811df417.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/param-saved-names-swapped/r-q-ado-misc.param-saved-names-swapped.575ec38ae1.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/param-saved-wrong-names/r-q-ado-misc.param-saved-wrong-names.7eaad60c99.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/param-markers-names/r-q-ado-misc.param-markers-names.5f7d196265.a1.json`; earlier `evidence/r-ado-1b/param-create-forms/r-ado-1b.param-create-forms.0492011378.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/param-refresh-after-text-change/r-ado-1b.param-refresh-after-text-change.f4ed5d8f03.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/param-value-tag-matrix/r-ado-1b.param-value-tag-matrix.1ba10384f9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/param-mutate-after-append/r-ado-1b.param-mutate-after-append.a7826b61ff.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-params-saved-query/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-params-saved-query.597c15efa9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/param-decimal-scale-fresh/r-fix-wire-ado.param-decimal-scale-fresh.d0d5c4e4ec.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/param-edge/r-oledb-odbc.param-edge.e84aa08f05.a1.json`. Discriminator: the swapped-order run, whose values (4 then 2) make position give an empty set and name give 2..4. The earlier "appending pA before pB fails Unspecified error" in `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/param-order-binding/r-oledb-odbc.param-order-binding.92ef1f32bf.a1.json` is explained by position: the first appended parameter there was a Text where the query's first parameter is Long.
- **Open**: arrays with gaps or non-zero bounds and Output directions through a real output parameter (builder has no array argument, T); ADO-PARAM-EVENT (T).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-RESULTS: row, empty, closed and refused result forms, NextRecordset, timeout values
- **Claim**: A row SELECT through Connection.Execute returns an open forward-only recordset (State 1, CursorType 0, LockType 1, RecordCount -1); an empty result is open with BOF and EOF true and the full field list (also `QEmpty`); an action statement returns a recordset with State 0 (closed); `adExecuteNoRecords` (129) returns a null interface pointer. Two statements in one text are refused 0x80040E14 "Characters found after end of SQL statement" (SQLState 3000) and the tail is not executed. `Recordset.NextRecordset` fails 0x800A0CB3 "Current provider does not support returning multiple recordsets from a single execution", on an open recordset and also on a closed one (not 0x800A0E78). `Recordset.Requery()` with no argument works (State 1, EOF false, first row ID 1 after a MoveNext). `Execute` slots: a value in the RecordsAffected slot or a String Options is accepted, Options 12345 gives 0x800A0BB9; `Open "T"` with adCmdText fails 0x80040E14, `"EXEC QAll"` works and `{call QAll}` fails; a Command with an explicit ActiveConnection refuses `Recordset.Open` with 0x800A0E7B. `Connection.CommandTimeout` defaults to 30 and accepts 0, 1, 99999, an I2 and the string "5" (read back 5), and refuses -1 (0x800A0BB9, value unchanged); a Command takes 30 whatever the Connection had (20 set before, 7 set after), and Command and Connection timeouts do not follow each other.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/results-nextrecordset-requery/r-q-ado-misc.results-nextrecordset-requery.d383798dae.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/results-timeout-values/r-q-ado-misc.results-timeout-values.5c3656f4e3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-execute-results-rows/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-execute-results-rows.00bec5d592.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-execute-update-compound/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-execute-update-compound.b25c1ab63f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-execute-action/r-str-ado-act.ado-execute-action.f337c92ed7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/cmd-execute-args/r-ado-1b.cmd-execute-args.77951ef251.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-source-kinds/r-ado-1b.rs-source-kinds.8238995ad3.a1.json`.
- **Open (T)**: whether a CommandTimeout cancels a delayed phase: the seven-way join under timeout 1 returned its full value and no elapsed time was recorded; it needs a measured lock delay.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-CURSOR: requested versus effective tuple, inheritance, visibility, CacheSize
- **Claim**: On a writable ACE connection the requested (location, type, lock) becomes: server forward-only read-only 0,1,2 (RecordCount -1, `MoveLast` 0x80040E24); server keyset optimistic 1,3,2 (RecordCount 4); server dynamic pessimistic is delivered as keyset (1,2,2); server static read-only 3,1,2; client static batch-optimistic 3,4,3; client forward-only 3,1,3; `adCmdTable` and `adCmdTableDirect` keyset 1,1,2. Client lock 2 reads back as 4; a server static request with an updatable lock reads back as keyset; on a read-mode connection updatable locks are delivered as LockType 1 and Update/AddNew/Delete fail 0x800A0CB3. A Recordset inherits the Connection's CursorLocation at Open (3 turns forward-only and keyset requests into static, RecordCount 7). Paging: PageSize 10 by default, AbsolutePage 99 gives position -3 with EOF, 0/-1 and PageSize 0 fail 0x800A0BB9, MaxRecords is ignored. Changes through the same Connection: a keyset shows an update at once, including rows inside its cache window (CacheSize 4 and 1 give the same reads, the unvisited rows 2, 3, 8 and 9 all read their new values) and a deleted row errors 0x80040E23; inserts are not visible; static recordsets show nothing; `Requery` shows all. Changes by another connection (update ID, insert, delete ID, in two rounds): a server keyset and a dynamic recordset (RecordCount 10 and -1) show the old values at once, show the update after 8 s and read the deleted row as an error (hr 0x80020009 on its fields), and do not show the insert; a server static and a client static recordset show nothing until `Requery`, which after 8 s shows update, delete and insert in all four. Both rounds gave the same result at both time points.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB; the 0 s and 8 s observations repeat in two rounds; the exact lag between 0 and 8 s is not bracketed (the earlier DAO records put it near 7 s).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/cursor-external-visibility/r-q-ado-misc.cursor-external-visibility.2f25fa0c3d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/cursor-cachesize-window/r-q-ado-misc.cursor-cachesize-window.50e555f741.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-rs-cursors-writable/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-rs-cursors-writable.f73a063b5c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-rs-cursors/r-str-ado-act.ado-rs-cursors.002e5fa5c5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/prop-rowset-requests/r-oledb-odbc.prop-rowset-requests.2862dd72da.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-lock-matrix/r-ado-1b.rs-lock-matrix.66f89c7e78.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/conn-cursorlocation-inherit/r-ado-1b.conn-cursorlocation-inherit.30d21b33cb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-readonly-mode-edit/r-ado-1b.rs-readonly-mode-edit.79357bf4a7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-paging/r-ado-1b.rs-paging.726129e683.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-cache-same-connection/r-ado-1b.rs-cache-same-connection.24054dcd7f.a1.json`. Discriminator for the earlier "others' writes invisible even after Requery" (`evidence/r-oledb-odbc/update-others-visible/r-oledb-odbc.update-others-visible.f1d10465be.a1.json`): a Requery issued after 8 s shows them, so that record is a lag, not a permanent invisibility (it issued its Requery without a pause).
- **Open**: the precise lag and whether Requery before 7 s shows the change (one-line: a pause sweep between 0 and 8 s); re-reading a visited cached row after a same-connection update.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-EDIT: edit states, automatic posting and cancel
- **Claim**: On a server keyset (optimistic) recordset an assigned Field.Value puts EditMode 1 and `Value` shows the proposal while `OriginalValue` keeps the stored value; `Update` returns EditMode 0 and OriginalValue becomes the new value; Move (MoveNext, MovePrevious) posts the edit; `CancelUpdate` restores; `AddNew`+`Update` yields the generated ID. On a client batch-optimistic recordset Find, Filter, Sort, Resync, MoveNext, MoveFirst, AddNew and Delete commit a pending edit locally, while `Requery`, `Close` and Clone-then-Close fail 0x800A0C93 and leave EditMode 1. `Update(name, value)` and `AddNew(name, value)` work; an unknown name or Empty gives 0x800A0CC1, a Missing name 0x800A0BB9, "abc" into a Long 0x80020005. A failed put (odd-byte text 0x80040E21, 256-character text, out-of-range numerics 0x8002000A) leaves the edit open; the following `Update` then fails ("Multiple-step OLE DB operation generated errors") without ending the recordset, and later rows write normally.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-edit-fresh/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-edit-fresh.d0143e1f67.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-write-values-numeric/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-write-values-numeric.2dd7d13095.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-write-values-text-binary/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-write-values-text-binary.b6e3456443.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/edit-pending-ops/r-ado-1b.edit-pending-ops.b69e9de040.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/edit-update-args/r-ado-1b.edit-update-args.645de455f0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/fields-assign-conversion/r-ado-1b.fields-assign-conversion.8d73c73cc5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-readonly-mode-edit/r-ado-1b.rs-readonly-mode-edit.79357bf4a7.a1.json`.
- **Open**: `Update(array, array)` forms (builder has no array argument, T); edits during events.


### R-ADO-1 ADO-VALUES: value channels, conflict reporting and Update Criteria
- **Claim**: Server keyset optimistic, ID 1 set to 100, edited to 120 and changed to 110 by a second connection: before the conflict `Value` 120 and `OriginalValue` 100; `UnderlyingValue` is refused 0x800A0CB3 ("Current provider does not support refreshing underlying values") before and after Update and after CancelUpdate; `Update` fails 0x80040E21 (SQLState 3197, "you and another user are attempting to change the same data at the same time") and EditMode stays 1; after the failure `OriginalValue` reads 110 (the external value) while `Value` stays 120; `CancelUpdate` gives EditMode 0 with Value and OriginalValue 110; a following edit to 130 and `Update` succeeds (EditMode 0, Value and OriginalValue 130). Client static batch-optimistic, same sequence: `Update` is local (EditMode 1, Value 120, OriginalValue 100, UnderlyingValue 100); `UpdateBatch` fails 0x80040E38 (Microsoft Cursor Engine, "Row cannot be located for updating", native 32) with the channels unchanged; `Resync(adAffectAll, adResyncUnderlyingValues)` makes UnderlyingValue 110 (Value 120, OriginalValue 100); the second `UpdateBatch` then succeeds with Value, OriginalValue and UnderlyingValue 120; a later edit to 130 and `UpdateBatch` succeeds (all three 130). Update Criteria (read 2 = adCriteriaUpdCols by default, set 0 to 3 per recordset), with the same column changed externally (100 to 120 against external 110): 0 (key) overwrites without error (Value and OriginalValue 120); 1 (all columns), 2 (updated columns) and 3 (timestamp) fail 0x80040E38 and leave Value 120, OriginalValue 100. With a different column changed externally criteria 0 and 2 apply and 1 and 3 fail (earlier record). A row deleted externally fails 0x80004005 "Record is deleted" with Status 2 and Value/OriginalValue/UnderlyingValue as read.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB; the server and client runs are separate probes; the criteria values were asked one per fresh recordset and row.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/values-witness-keyset/r-q-ado-misc.values-witness-keyset.d13e93ae91.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/values-witness-client-batch/r-q-ado-misc.values-witness-client-batch.226dc9e511.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/values-witness-criteria/r-q-ado-misc.values-witness-criteria.93d8b10f55.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-update-criteria/r-ado-1b.batch-update-criteria.8b6dda4158.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/values-keyset-conflict/r-ado-1b.values-keyset-conflict.38ab9f531d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-conflict-delete/r-ado-1b.batch-conflict-delete.1d4d89d6ea.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/batch-conflict-delete-readback/r-fix-wire-ado.batch-conflict-delete-readback.5b4b34d50a.a1.json`. Not used as evidence: the stored-value reads at the end of the new records (defect a, no pause) and the Field `Status` reads (defect b).
- **Open**: row-level `Recordset.Status` flags during these steps; stored values confirmed after a pause.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-BATCH: staged changes are all attempted; conflicts at first, middle and last row
- **Claim**: `UpdateBatch(adAffectAll)` attempts every staged row, in staging order, and reports a failure without stopping: with the first staged row conflicting (external change) rows 2 and 3 are stored (OriginalValue updated) and row 1 keeps Status 2050; a duplicate key in the middle row leaves rows 1 and 3 stored and row 2 unchanged (statuses: failing row 2, others 8); with the last staged row conflicting, rows 1 and 2 are stored (read by an independent connection) and row 3 fails 0x80040E38 (native 32). A deleted row fails 0x80004005 "Record is deleted". After `Resync` (underlying) the second UpdateBatch completes without error in each case (earlier records read the stored values through the same recordset; for the last-row case the independent reader still showed the external value, no pause, defect a). Referential failures give "related record is required" / "includes related records"; a duplicate resubmission gives -2147467259 and does not resend accepted rows; generated inserts and mixed staging orders ran without failing calls.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/batch-conflict-last/r-q-ado-misc.batch-conflict-last.db26b48c2f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-conflict-first/r-ado-1b.batch-conflict-first.b4ff3fae6a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-conflict-middle/r-ado-1b.batch-conflict-middle.7702a784fb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-fk-failure/r-ado-1b.batch-fk-failure.9933a7a86b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-resubmit/r-ado-1b.batch-resubmit.9f9796ac6b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-status-staging/r-ado-1b.batch-status-staging.28795ef6df.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-mixed-generated/r-ado-1b.batch-mixed-generated.52031cc21f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-batch/r-str-ado-act.ado-batch.47c0bca9d0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/batch-conflict-delete-readback/r-fix-wire-ado.batch-conflict-delete-readback.5b4b34d50a.a1.json`. Correction: the draft said "stops at the first failure"; `batch-conflict-first` (rows 2 and 3 stored after row 1 failed) and `ado-batch` (row 3 stored after row 2 failed) contradict that. Discriminator for "the failure is the reading connection": a fresh third connection and DAO read the data (`batch-conflict-delete-readback`).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-BATCH-SCOPE (part): adAffectCurrent, group by constant filter, Update Criteria
- **Claim**: `UpdateBatch(adAffectCurrent)` submits only the current row: with rows 1 to 3 staged and the cursor on row 2, the pending-filter count afterwards is 2 and the later `UpdateBatch(adAffectAll)` finishes the rest (rows 1 and 2 read changed by the independent reader; the third row's final value was not visible in that read, defect a). `Filter = adFilterPendingRecords (1)` over a pending Delete, a pending update and a pending AddNew gives RecordCount 3, and `UpdateBatch(adAffectGroup)` then stores all three (row 4 gone, row 5 updated, new row 11 present in the independent read, no errors). The empty-group and string-filter refusals (0x800A0C93) and the Update Criteria matrix are in the earlier records and in ADO-VALUES.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/batch-scope-current/r-q-ado-misc.batch-scope-current.0644b593d8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/batch-scope-pending-deletes/r-q-ado-misc.batch-scope-pending-deletes.8b3beda486.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/values-witness-criteria/r-q-ado-misc.values-witness-criteria.93d8b10f55.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/batch-scope-group-pending-constant/r-fix-wire-ado.batch-scope-group-pending-constant.edad1c330c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/batch-scope-group-string-filter/r-fix-wire-ado.batch-scope-group-string-filter.a8203914f2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/batch-scope-group-unposted-edit/r-fix-wire-ado.batch-scope-group-unposted-edit.390bbf0159.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/batch-update-criteria/r-ado-1b.batch-update-criteria.8b6dda4158.a1.json`.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-TXN: connection-level nesting, rollback scope, retaining
- **Claim**: `BeginTrans` returns the nesting level (1, 2, 3); an inner commit is undone by an outer rollback; a final Commit/Rollback with no transaction fails 0x8004D00E; Connection.Execute, Command.Execute, keyset Update and client UpdateBatch all revert on RollbackTrans, DDL (CREATE/ALTER/INSERT) included; `IsolationLevel = 1048576` is accepted but the next BeginTrans fails 0x8004D008 and later work autocommits; held server and client recordsets stay State 1 across commit and rollback (Preserve on Commit true; Preserve on Abort false for the two server cursors, true for client static); with Attributes 393216 the transaction stays open after commit or rollback and `Close` fails 0x800A0CAE; after rollback a keyset shows the stored row and `Requery` restores client caches; `Resync` is refused 0x800A0CB3 on keyset and server static recordsets and works on client ones.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-ado-1b/txn-nesting-three/r-ado-1b.txn-nesting-three.00ca433b23.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/txn-rollback-scope-objects/r-ado-1b.txn-rollback-scope-objects.64de5c2b49.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/txn-client-cache-rollback/r-ado-1b.txn-client-cache-rollback.ed5b011515.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/txn-client-cache-rollback-distinct-rows/r-fix-wire-ado.txn-client-cache-rollback-distinct-rows.ce457accdf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-txn/r-str-ado-act.ado-txn.eb75f1792d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/txn-held-rows/r-oledb-odbc.txn-held-rows.91a0129038.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/txn-ddl-rollback/r-oledb-odbc.txn-ddl-rollback.b6e0df0330.a1.json`.
- **Open**: failed successor creation on a retaining route; clone closure.


### R-ADO-1 ADO-VIEW: Filter and Sort grammar, LIKE wildcards, Sort clearing
- **Claim**: Filter constants 0, 1, 2 and 3 work (RecordCount 7, 3, 0 and 1 for the fixture), "" matches all, and 99, -1, "1", Empty, Null, R8 1 and BOOL -1 fail 0x800A0BB9. String filters accept `>`, AND, OR, `<>`, `[S]`, case-insensitive equality and `LIKE`; NOT, parentheses, IN, BETWEEN, `==`, `!=`, UPPER() and arithmetic are rejected 0x800A0BB9; `L = NULL` matches Null rows and `L IS NULL` fails. LIKE: `*` and `%` are both wildcards and behave the same (`T LIKE 'a*'` and `'a%'` give 2 rows, `'*a*'` and `'%a%'` give 9), and a wildcard must be trailing or on both ends: a leading-only pattern (`'%a'`, `'%1'`, `'*1'`) is rejected 0x800A0BB9 whichever wildcard it uses. This resolves the draft's contradiction: `ado-view` used a trailing `%`, `view-filter-operators` a leading-only `'%a'`. Other pattern characters: `'alph?'` matched no row and `'alph_'` matched 2 rows (alpha and alpha2; its mechanism was not separated). Sort: unknown field 0x800A0CC1, "L DESC DESC" 0x800A0BB9, Memo and Binary fields 0x80040E60, trailing comma, lower case and `[S]` accepted; `Sort "L DESC, ID"` returns IDs 4, 8, 3, 7, 2, 6, 10, 1, 5, 9 and `Sort ""` restores the original order 1 to 10; `Sort` fails 0x800A0CB3 on every server cursor and on Execute results while Filter works on all; `Find` with `ID = = 3` fails and leaves the position.
- **Conditions**: client static recordset of K (10 rows) for the new records, ACCDB.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/view-like-percent-sort-clear/r-q-ado-misc.view-like-percent-sort-clear.6c886a25ff.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-filter-constants/r-ado-1b.view-filter-constants.68a67ea2d6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-filter-operators/r-ado-1b.view-filter-operators.1e0ea81dbc.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-filter-literals/r-ado-1b.view-filter-literals.f7f5edb1f8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-sort-grammar/r-ado-1b.view-sort-grammar.5a49f6f4bf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-sort-server/r-ado-1b.view-sort-server.09ed08dce8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/view-find-client/r-ado-1b.view-find-client.d7c719d9b9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-view/r-str-ado-act.ado-view.d257b3e165.a1.json`.
- **Open**: why `'alph_'` matches two rows; bookmark-array filters (array argument: T).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-BOOK (part): raw bookmark forms and position values
- **Claim**: Forward-only recordsets do not support bookmarks (`Supports(adBookmark)` 0, Bookmark 0x800A0CB3, `MoveLast` 0x80040E24, AbsolutePosition and RecordCount -1). Keyset Bookmark is VT_R8 (512, 513, ... 518 at rows 1, 2, 7); server and client static are VT_R8 1, 2, 7 equal to the row position. Past the end AbsolutePosition is -3 and Bookmark 0x800A0BCD; before the start -2. Assigning a Long, String, Empty or Null bookmark fails 0x800A0BB9, a 4-byte array and R8 2 fail 0x80040E0E "Not a valid bookmark" on keyset. A Clone starts at row 1 with Filter 0 and Sort ""; after the parent is filtered to one row the clone still has RecordCount 4.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/book-position-readback/r-fix-wire-ado.book-position-readback.80fff259e9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/book-raw-forms/r-ado-1b.book-raw-forms.30a727ca1b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-view/r-str-ado-act.ado-view.d257b3e165.a1.json`.
- **Open**: runtime bookmarks passed back (Filter by bookmark array, CompareBookmarks, clone/original exchange) need a VARIANT array argument: T. The earlier "static bookmark 3 at RecordCount 7" oddity was resolved by `book-position-readback`.


### R-ADO-1 ADO-RESYNC: Resync scope and values on a client batch recordset
- **Claim**: On a client static batch-optimistic recordset, after another connection updated ID 1, deleted ID 2 and inserted a row (8 s earlier): the recordset still shows the old rows; `Resync(adAffectAll, adResyncAllValues)` fails 0x80040E23 (Microsoft Cursor Engine, "Key value for this row was changed or deleted at the data store. The local row is now deleted"), yet the existing rows are refreshed (ID 1 reads 777) and the deleted row is dropped (RecordCount 9); the inserted row is not discovered; `Requery` discovers it (RecordCount 10, ID 1 reads 888 after a later change) and `Fields.Refresh` leaves Count 4. Scope matrix (external update of IDs 1 to 4, 8 s pause, cursor on row 2, `L` read): `Resync(adAffectCurrent, adResyncUnderlyingValues)` changes no Value; `Resync(adAffectCurrent, adResyncAllValues)` refreshes row 2 only; `Resync(adAffectGroup, ...)` without a Filter fails 0x800A0C93 and changes nothing; `Resync(adAffectAll, adResyncAllValues)` refreshes all rows. Earlier: on keyset and server static recordsets Resync fails 0x800A0CB3 and on a client static (read-only) recordset 0x80004005 "Insufficient base table information".
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/resync-client-external/r-q-ado-misc.resync-client-external.48ce593a95.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/resync-affect-matrix/r-q-ado-misc.resync-affect-matrix.807bb2db9e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/rs-cache-same-connection/r-ado-1b.rs-cache-same-connection.24054dcd7f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/txn-client-cache-rollback-distinct-rows/r-fix-wire-ado.txn-client-cache-rollback-distinct-rows.ce457accdf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/txn-client-cache-rollback/r-ado-1b.txn-client-cache-rollback.ed5b011515.a1.json`.
- **Still missing (F, one line)**: after `Resync(adAffectAll, adResyncUnderlyingValues)` on a clean row UnderlyingValue stayed at the earlier 777 although the source held 888, while on a pending-edit row the same call moved UnderlyingValue to the external value (ADO-VALUES): one record, unexplained, needs a repeat.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-BULK: GetRows and GetString shapes
- **Claim**: `GetRows(-1)` returns a variant array bounds [0..4]x[0..4] with the row values (Null stays Null); `GetRows(2, 1, "T")` returns two rows of one field and moves AbsolutePosition to 3; `GetRows(0)` fails 0x800A0C93; GetRows over a Decimal column returns all 10 rows (DAO returned 4); `GetString` with clip parameters renders Null as the given Null text and keeps embedded NUL; default `GetString` is tab/CR separated with Null as an empty string.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-SCHEMA: field metadata, refresh after DDL and schema routes
- **Claim**: ADO publishes ACE field types as: Long 3, Text 202 (size 10/255), Double 5, Currency 6, Date 7, Memo 203 (536870910), Binary 205 / 204, Yes/No 11, Short 2, Byte 17, Single 4, GUID 72, Decimal 131 (precision 38 for DAO-made fields, as declared for DDL columns). Decimal expressions: N, N+1, N+N, N/2, -N are adNumeric 131 (precision 18, scale 4); `N*1.5` is adDouble; Sum(N) precision 28 scale 4; Count(N) adInteger. After `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` with the table closed, a retained Fields collection reports the old count until Requery (client cursor: 5 after Refresh, 6 after Requery; on a keyset the ALTER itself fails 0x80040E14 "could not lock table" while the recordset is open); a new recordset sees the column. `Connection.OpenSchema(id)` with the restrictions argument omitted succeeds for 16 SchemaEnum values: 4, 5, 6, 8, 9, 10, 12, 16, 19, 20, 22, 23, 27, 28, 30 (67 rows) and 31 (16 rows), all forward-only (RecordCount -1) except those two; 0, 1, 2, 3, 7, 11, 13, 14, 15, 17, 18, 21, 24, 25, 26, 29 and 32 to 41 fail 0x800A0CB3 and 9999 fails 0x800A0BB9.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/schema-ids-no-restrictions/r-q-ado-misc.schema-ids-no-restrictions.f1f79a0408.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/fields-meta-mdb/r-ado-1b.fields-meta-mdb.b93c565d13.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-meta-decimal-expressions/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-meta-decimal-expressions.a7d2aad72e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-refresh-ddl-closed/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-refresh-ddl-closed.2b8ac47651.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-refresh-ddl-client-only/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-refresh-ddl-client-only.27000fbbd5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-refresh-ddl-keyset-only/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-refresh-ddl-keyset-only.fdd76b6a40.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-schema/r-str-ado-act.ado-schema.a2208b3039.a1.json`. Discriminator: the earlier "every other id refused" (`evidence/r-oledb-odbc/schema-others/r-oledb-odbc.schema-others.4ef914e3e7.a1.json`) passed scalar restrictions; without them ids 5, 8, 9, 10, 28, 30 and 31 are supported, so that earlier list is superseded.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-RECORD: Record.Open forms on the ACE provider
- **Claim**: None of the Record sources asked is supported by the ACE provider: `Record.Open("K", cn)` and `Record.Open("SELECT * FROM K", cn)` fail 0x800A0E7D ("The connection cannot be used to perform this operation"), `Record.Open(Command, cn)` and (earlier) `Record.Open(Recordset)` fail 0x800A0CB3, and the Record stays State 0 (its RecordType and ParentURL then fail 0x800A0E78). No URL, file or network source was asked (scope).
